public-easy-01 · Automated Filling Line Control · 10条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：e323c12383c6f19ff7d8c0bb8c661335d20df14a366b66c3f930a384b8313d5c

An automated filling line responsible for cleaning, filling, capping, and packaging bottles. Each step of the production line is controlled by PLC and triggered by signals from sensors and operators.
Control requirements:
When the sensor bottleSensor detects a bottle, it triggers the start of the cleaning pump and continues to work.
After cleaning is completed, the operator operates the button cleaningConfirmButton to trigger the cleaning completion signal, automatically starting the filling valve for filling.
After filling is completed, the operator operates the button fillingConfirmButton to trigger the filling completion signal, automatically starting the capping machine for capping.
After capping is completed, the operator operates the button cappingConfirmButton to trigger the capping completion signal, starting the packing machine for packaging.
After packaging is completed, the operator operates the button packingConfirmButton to trigger the packaging completion signal, lighting up the packaging completion indicator light.
After the operator picks up the packaged bottle, they press the confirmation button finishedButton to reset the indicator light and prepare for the next bottle's production cycle.
变量类型方向说明
bottleSensorBOOLinputThe bottle reaches the sensor, triggering a signal when a bottle is detected
cleaningConfirmButtonBOOLinputOperator confirmation button, used to confirm the completion of the cleaning process
fillingConfirmButtonBOOLinputOperator confirmation button, used to confirm the completion of the filling process
cappingConfirmButtonBOOLinputOperator confirmation button, used to confirm the completion of the capping process
packingConfirmButtonBOOLinputOperator confirmation button, used to confirm the completion of the packaging process
finishedButtonBOOLinputOperator confirmation button, used to confirm that the bottle has been picked up
Pump_MotorBOOLoutputControl output for the operation of the cleaning pump motor
Filling_ValveBOOLoutputControl output for the operation of the filling valve
Capping_MachineBOOLoutputControl output for the operation of the capping machine
Packing_MachineBOOLoutputControl output for the operation of the packing machine
Completion_LightBOOLoutputIndicator light for packaging completion

性质

  • S1 检测到瓶子且没有取瓶命令时，清洗泵必须在同一拍运行（R1）
    G(bottleSensor AND NOT finishedButton -> Pump_Motor)
  • S2 按下取瓶确认后，所有执行机构与完成灯都在同一拍复位（R6）
    G(finishedButton -> NOT Completion_Light AND NOT Packing_Machine AND NOT Capping_Machine AND NOT Filling_Valve AND NOT Pump_Motor)
  • S3 完成灯只允许在全线包装完成（包装机运行）时点亮（R5）
    G(Completion_Light -> Packing_Machine)
  • S4 灌装阀一开始工作就意味着清洗泵早已在运行，工序不会越级（R1/R2）
    G(rising(Filling_Valve) -> Pump_Motor)
  • S5 压盖机运行时灌装阀必然在运行，工序链条不会断开（R2/R3）
    G(Capping_Machine -> Filling_Valve)
  • N10-S6 11.10补充：已运行的 Pump_Motor 收到 cleaningConfirmButton 时应启动 Filling_Valve，取瓶复位优先。
    G((prev(Pump_Motor) AND cleaningConfirmButton AND NOT finishedButton) -> Filling_Valve)
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：已运行的 Filling_Valve 收到 fillingConfirmButton 时应启动 Capping_Machine，取瓶复位优先。
    G((prev(Filling_Valve) AND fillingConfirmButton AND NOT finishedButton) -> Capping_Machine)
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：已运行的 Capping_Machine 收到 cappingConfirmButton 时应启动 Packing_Machine，取瓶复位优先。
    G((prev(Capping_Machine) AND cappingConfirmButton AND NOT finishedButton) -> Packing_Machine)
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：已运行的 Packing_Machine 收到 packingConfirmButton 时应启动 Completion_Light，取瓶复位优先。
    G((prev(Packing_Machine) AND packingConfirmButton AND NOT finishedButton) -> Completion_Light)
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：启动的执行机构和完成灯保持到取瓶复位。
    G(NOT finishedButton -> ((prev(Pump_Motor) -> Pump_Motor) AND (prev(Filling_Valve) -> Filling_Valve) AND (prev(Capping_Machine) -> Capping_Machine) AND (prev(Packing_Machine) -> Packing_Machine) AND (prev(Completion_Light) -> Completion_Light)))
  • N10-S11 11.10补充：灌装、压盖、包装、完成灯的启动必须分别有工序确认；压盖及后续工序启动前一拍应已有上游工序。
    G((rising(Filling_Valve) -> cleaningConfirmButton) AND (rising(Capping_Machine) -> (prev(Filling_Valve) AND fillingConfirmButton)) AND (rising(Packing_Machine) -> (prev(Capping_Machine) AND cappingConfirmButton)) AND (rising(Completion_Light) -> (prev(Packing_Machine) AND packingConfirmButton)))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-easy-02 · Dual-Word Edge Detection · 11条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：2b857660e15ae9e873f0b8c7e39671fe9ef77625e626d049fe0e1dbc6d848146

Write a function block FB that checks for rising and falling edges in each bit of a dual-word (DWord) input value every cycle. The function block should be able to detect and report changes, rising edges, and falling edges in the input value.
变量类型方向说明
valueDWORDinputThe input value to be checked for changes and edge triggering
hasChangedBOOLoutputTrue indicates that the input value has changed
hasRisingEdgesBOOLoutputTrue indicates that rising edges have occurred in the input value
risingBitsDWORDoutputThe bit stream of rising edges, where each bit indicates whether the corresponding input bit has experienced a rising edge (1 for a rising edge, 0 for no rising edge)
noOfRisingBitsUSINToutputThe number of rising edges in the input value
hasFallingEdgesBOOLoutputTrue indicates that falling edges have occurred in the input value
fallingBitsDWORDoutputThe bit stream of falling edges, where each bit indicates whether the corresponding input bit has experienced a falling edge (1 for a falling edge, 0 for no falling edge)
noOfFallingBitsUSINToutputThe number of falling edges in the input value

性质

  • S1 hasChanged 必须等于两类边沿位流是否有非零位（R1 的变化判断与 R2/R4 自洽）
    G(hasChanged = (risingBits <> 0 OR fallingBits <> 0))
  • S2 hasRisingEdges 必须与上升沿位流 risingBits 是否非零一致（R1）
    G(hasRisingEdges = (risingBits <> 0))
  • S3 hasFallingEdges 必须与下降沿位流 fallingBits 是否非零一致（R2）
    G(hasFallingEdges = (fallingBits <> 0))
  • S4 整字由 0 变成非 0（整字上升沿）时，必须报告上升沿（R1）
    G(rising(value <> 0) -> hasRisingEdges)
  • S5 整字由非 0 变成 0（整字下降沿）时，必须报告下降沿（R2）
    G(falling(value <> 0) -> hasFallingEdges)
  • S6 上一拍与本拍的输入不同（整字变化）时，本拍必须报告 hasChanged；首拍的前值约定为上电清零值 0（与 PLC 存储区初值一致）
    G((value <> prev(value)) -> hasChanged)
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：上升沿位掩码逐位对应本拍与上拍的 DWORD 输入。
    G(risingBits = (value AND NOT prev(value)))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：上升沿数量等于该掩码全部 32 位的一位数。
    G(noOfRisingBits = (((risingBits SHR 0) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 1) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 2) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 3) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 4) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 5) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 6) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 7) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 8) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 9) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 10) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 11) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 12) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 13) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 14) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 15) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 16) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 17) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 18) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 19) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 20) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 21) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 22) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 23) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 24) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 25) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 26) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 27) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 28) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 29) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 30) AND 1) + ((risingBits SHR 31) AND 1)))
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：下降沿位掩码逐位对应本拍与上拍的 DWORD 输入。
    G(fallingBits = (prev(value) AND NOT value))
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：下降沿数量等于该掩码全部 32 位的一位数。
    G(noOfFallingBits = (((fallingBits SHR 0) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 1) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 2) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 3) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 4) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 5) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 6) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 7) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 8) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 9) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 10) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 11) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 12) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 13) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 14) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 15) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 16) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 17) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 18) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 19) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 20) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 21) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 22) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 23) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 24) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 25) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 26) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 27) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 28) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 29) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 30) AND 1) + ((fallingBits SHR 31) AND 1)))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

tc-02sequence1{"value": 15}{}{"hasChanged": true, "hasRisingEdges": true, "risingBits": 15, "noOfRisingBits": 4, "hasFallingEdges": false, "fallingBits": 0, "noOfFallingBits": 0}部分承接上一条：0 -> 16#0000000F，低 4 位全部上升（R1/R2/R4）

tc-04sequence1{"value": 240}{}{"hasChanged": true, "hasRisingEdges": true, "risingBits": 240, "noOfRisingBits": 4, "hasFallingEdges": true, "fallingBits": 15, "noOfFallingBits": 4}部分承接上一条：16#0F -> 16#F0，低 4 位下降、高 4 位上升，两类边沿同拍出现

public-easy-03 · Alarm Handling · 8条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：76dc8f206d052971b4012b248052d8f85416106bc125d145e9ac4e11a3e983dd

In industrial automation systems, monitoring the status of equipment is crucial. When equipment parameters exceed the normal range, the system needs to issue an alarm. To avoid frequent triggering of alarms due to minor fluctuations in parameters, an alarm management system with hysteresis function needs to be implemented.
When Value is greater than hiLevel, trigger high alarm hiAlarm.
When Value is less than hiLevel-hystWindow, exit high alarm hiAlarm.
When Value is less than loLevel, trigger low alarm loAlarm.
When Value is greater than loLevel+hystWindow, exit low alarm loAlarm.
If loLevel is greater than or equal to hiLevel, output error error=true, status=16#8001, and exit the program
If the hysteresis window value hystWindow is greater than or equal to the deviation range between hiLevel and loLevel, output error error=true, status=16#8002
变量类型方向说明
ValueREALinputInput value
hiLevelREALinputHigh limit alarm value
loLevelREALinputLow limit alarm value
hystWindowREALinputHysteresis window
hiAlarmBOOLoutputHigh limit alarm
loAlarmBOOLoutputLow limit alarm
errorBOOLoutputFALSE: No error occurred;<br>TRUE: Error occurred during execution of FB
statusWORDoutputStatus code

性质

  • S1 loLevel 不低于 hiLevel 时必须在同一拍报 error 且 status=16#8001（R5）
    G(loLevel >= hiLevel -> error AND status = 32769)
  • S2 上下限正常但回差不小于量程时，必须在同一拍报 error 且 status=16#8002（R6）
    G((loLevel < hiLevel) AND (hystWindow >= (hiLevel - loLevel)) -> error AND status = 32770)
  • S3 无错误时 Value 高于高限必须置高限报警（R1）
    G(NOT error AND Value > hiLevel -> hiAlarm)
  • S4 无错误时 Value 低于 hiLevel-hystWindow 必须复位高限报警（R2）
    G(NOT error AND Value < (hiLevel - hystWindow) -> NOT hiAlarm)
  • S5 无错误时 Value 低于低限必须置低限报警（R3）
    G(NOT error AND Value < loLevel -> loAlarm)
  • S6 无错误时 Value 高于 loLevel+hystWindow 必须复位低限报警（R4）
    G(NOT error AND Value > (loLevel + hystWindow) -> NOT loAlarm)
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：回差宽度非负且小于正常区间宽度、上下限顺序正确时，错误位清除且状态码为零。
    G((hystWindow >= 0.0 AND loLevel < hiLevel AND hystWindow < hiLevel - loLevel) -> (NOT error AND status = 0))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：高报警在闭合回差带内保持原状态。
    G((NOT error AND Value <= hiLevel AND Value >= hiLevel - hystWindow) -> (hiAlarm <-> prev(hiAlarm)))
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：低报警在闭合回差带内保持原状态。
    G((NOT error AND Value >= loLevel AND Value <= loLevel + hystWindow) -> (loAlarm <-> prev(loAlarm)))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

编号组拍数有效输入初态预期断言范围说明
tc-01sequence1{"Value": 70.5, "hiLevel": 70.0, "loLevel": 20.0, "hystWindow": 5.0}{}{"hiAlarm": true, "loAlarm": false, "error": false}部分初始态：Value 略高于高限 -> 置高限报警，低限不报警，无错（R1）

public-easy-04 · Jam Pump Debouncing and Runtime Maintenance · 7条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：92070533bc6599e9a4c44f7b0ac29825f616e1f7e703fb346ac3702d433aeb5e

Control a jam transfer pump on a food production line, including viscosity-signal debouncing, emergency-stop priority, runtime accumulation and a maintenance-due indication.

1. Accept the viscosity condition only after VISCOSITY_OK has remained TRUE for DebounceTime. Invalidate the condition immediately when the input becomes FALSE.
2. Allow VFD_RUN to be TRUE only when PASTE_E_STOP_NC is TRUE and the viscosity condition is valid. An emergency stop immediately disables the run output and clears any incomplete debounce timing.
3. Accumulate PUMP_RUNTIME_ACC using the actual invocation interval CycleTime only while VFD_RUN is TRUE. Preserve the accumulated time while the pump is stopped.
4. Set PUMP_MAINT_DUE when accumulated runtime reaches 500 hours. This indication is informational and must not affect pump operation or the safety permissive.
5. At power-up, initialize the run output to OFF, accumulated runtime to zero and the maintenance indication to FALSE.
变量类型方向说明
VISCOSITY_OKBOOLinputRaw signal indicating that viscosity is within the permitted range.
PASTE_E_STOP_NCBOOLinputNormally closed emergency-stop circuit; TRUE permits operation and FALSE means emergency stop.
DebounceTimeTIMEinputRequired continuous valid duration for the viscosity signal; must be positive.
CycleTimeTIMEinputActual elapsed interval represented by this invocation; must be positive.
VFD_RUNBOOLoutputPump variable-frequency-drive run command.
PUMP_MAINT_DUEBOOLoutputMaintenance indication when accumulated runtime reaches 500 hours.
PUMP_RUNTIME_ACCLINToutputAccumulated enabled runtime, using a long-duration time type.

性质

  • S1 当急停回路断开（PASTE_E_STOP_NC=FALSE）时，VFD_RUN 必须为 FALSE。
    G(NOT PASTE_E_STOP_NC -> NOT VFD_RUN)
  • S2 当粘度原始信号为 FALSE 时，去抖条件立即失效，VFD_RUN 必须为 FALSE。
    G(NOT VISCOSITY_OK -> NOT VFD_RUN)
  • S3 当维护提示 PUMP_MAINT_DUE 为 TRUE 时，累计运行时间必须已达到 500。
    G(PUMP_MAINT_DUE -> PUMP_RUNTIME_ACC >= 500)
  • S4 当 VFD_RUN 为 FALSE 时，累计运行时间必须与上一拍相同（停机保持，不清零也不增加）。
    G(NOT VFD_RUN -> PUMP_RUNTIME_ACC = prev(PUMP_RUNTIME_ACC))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-easy-05 · Weld Gap Alarm and Startup Inhibition · 7条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：af8e263a03889522cb7fb2c07a2978eccc14e0fb6f771358440c4d30faadafec

Control a weld-gap alarm with power-up monitoring inhibition, input debouncing during normal monitoring and emergency-stop priority.

1. Gap_Exceeded indicates that the scanner has detected a weld gap exceeding the permitted 1.5 mm value.
2. Begin with a five-scan initialization inhibition period after power-up. During this period, Operator_Alert is FALSE, Weld_Soft_Start_Active is TRUE, and gap-signal debounce time does not accumulate.
3. Resume gap monitoring after initialization. Allow the alarm only after Gap_Exceeded has remained TRUE for three consecutive monitoring scans. When the signal becomes FALSE, switch off the alarm and clear debounce timing.
4. When Weld_E_Stop_NC is FALSE, the emergency stop takes priority over initialization and normal monitoring. Operator_Alert must immediately become FALSE and any incomplete debounce timing must be cleared.
5. Weld_Soft_Start_Active indicates only the power-up inhibition period; it does not represent a continuous speed ramp.
In this scan-count profile the first five scans are inhibited, including emergency-stop scans. DebounceTime is retained for interface compatibility and does not set the threshold.
变量类型方向说明
Gap_ExceededBOOLinputScanner indication that the gap exceeds 1.5 mm.
Weld_E_Stop_NCBOOLinputTRUE means the emergency stop is inactive; FALSE means it is active.
DebounceTimeTIMEinputCompatibility input; three consecutive monitoring scans are used.
Operator_AlertBOOLoutputAudible and visual alarm command.
Weld_Soft_Start_ActiveBOOLoutputMonitoring-inhibition status during the 5-second power-up initialization.

性质

  • S1 当上电抑制期指示为 TRUE 时，报警必须为 FALSE（抑制期内屏蔽报警）。
    G(Weld_Soft_Start_Active -> NOT Operator_Alert)
  • S2 当急停按下（Weld_E_Stop_NC=FALSE）时，报警必须为 FALSE（急停优先）。
    G(NOT Weld_E_Stop_NC -> NOT Operator_Alert)
  • S3 报警为 TRUE 时必须同时满足：间隙超差为 TRUE 且急停未按下。
    G(Operator_Alert -> (Gap_Exceeded AND Weld_E_Stop_NC))
  • S4 当间隙超差信号刚由 FALSE 变为 TRUE 的那一拍，不许立刻报警（去抖至少还要再等 2 拍）。
    G(Gap_Exceeded AND NOT prev(Gap_Exceeded) -> NOT Operator_Alert)
  • S5 上电抑制期为 5 个扫描周期、监视期需连续 3 个扫描周期超差才报警（以周期数代替 DebounceTime，由 e05-1..e05-4 用例验证的数据性质）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：仅在最近连续三拍都处于正常监测、信号超差且急停释放时报警。
    G(Operator_Alert <-> ((Gap_Exceeded AND Weld_E_Stop_NC AND NOT Weld_Soft_Start_Active) AND prev(((Gap_Exceeded AND Weld_E_Stop_NC AND NOT Weld_Soft_Start_Active) AND prev((Gap_Exceeded AND Weld_E_Stop_NC AND NOT Weld_Soft_Start_Active))))))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-easy-06 · Aeration Blower Delayed Start · 7条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：7d94fd204642001fef5977405191039a63d39024c23ac08e5fa590fc28dd9ef7

Control a wastewater-treatment aeration blower with a low-dissolved-oxygen demand, a 5-second start delay, emergency-stop priority and start-cycle counting.

1. Start delay timing when both DO_LOW and AERATION_E_STOP_NC are TRUE. Allow BLOWER_RUN to become TRUE only after these conditions have remained satisfied for 5 seconds.
2. If DO_LOW becomes FALSE or an emergency stop occurs, immediately disable BLOWER_RUN and reset the start timer, whether timing is incomplete or complete. A new demand must wait the full 5 seconds again.
3. Increment AERATION_BLOWER_CYCLE_COUNT once on each FALSE-to-TRUE transition of BLOWER_RUN. Do not count repeatedly during continuous operation.
4. At power-up, initialize the blower to OFF and the start count to zero. The start delay gates a BOOL enable; no continuous speed ramp is required.
变量类型方向说明
DO_LOWBOOLinputDissolved oxygen is below its threshold and aeration is needed.
AERATION_E_STOP_NCBOOLinputTRUE means the emergency stop is inactive; FALSE means it is active.
BLOWER_RUNBOOLoutputBlower motor-contactor run command.
AERATION_BLOWER_CYCLE_COUNTUINToutputCount of actual blower starts.

性质

  • S1 当急停按下（AERATION_E_STOP_NC=FALSE）时，鼓风机运行命令必须为 FALSE。
    G(NOT AERATION_E_STOP_NC -> NOT BLOWER_RUN)
  • S2 当低氧需求为 FALSE 时，鼓风机运行命令必须为 FALSE。
    G(NOT DO_LOW -> NOT BLOWER_RUN)
  • S3 鼓风机连续运行期间（本拍与上一拍都在运行）启动计数不得增加。
    G(BLOWER_RUN AND prev(BLOWER_RUN) -> AERATION_BLOWER_CYCLE_COUNT = prev(AERATION_BLOWER_CYCLE_COUNT))
  • S4 上电初始化后 5 个扫描周期内不得启动（以周期数代替 5 秒延时），且计数只在运行命上升沿加 1（由 e06-1..e06-7 用例验证的数据性质）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S5 11.10补充：连续五拍低氧且急停释放才启动，需求中断须重新等待。
    G(BLOWER_RUN <-> ((DO_LOW AND AERATION_E_STOP_NC) AND prev(((DO_LOW AND AERATION_E_STOP_NC) AND prev(((DO_LOW AND AERATION_E_STOP_NC) AND prev(((DO_LOW AND AERATION_E_STOP_NC) AND prev((DO_LOW AND AERATION_E_STOP_NC))))))))))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-hard-01 · Material Proportioning Process Control · 29条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：b4f6ee24e38a854127555e75cfbe03d47c7f370a0e20c0569684db160153667a

Design a PLC program to control a material proportioning process that requires mixing three types of materials according to specific requirements and then delivering them to the next process through valve D. The system needs to support both manual and automatic operation modes and be able to safely stop all equipment when an emergency stop signal is triggered.
Control requirements:
Emergency Stop Logic: The estop(=FALSE) signal is used for emergency stopping of all equipment.
Manual Mode: opeMode=false, manually control the start/stop of valves A, B, C, D, and the mixing motor.
Automatic Mode: opeMode=true, equipment operates automatically according to the process flow.
Implement the emergency stop logic to ensure that all equipment stops immediately when the emergency stop signal is triggered.
In manual mode, manually control the start/stop of valves and the mixing motor.
For example, pressing the valve A start button valveAStart (triggering a rising edge) causes valve A to run (valveARun=TRUE); pressing the valve A start button valveAStart again (triggering a rising edge) stops valve A (valveARun=FALSE).
In automatic mode, implement the following control logic based on the process flow:
Press the autoStart button to start the automatic process, first opening valve A.
When the liquid level sensor levelSensor reaches 50cm, close valve A and open valve B.
When the liquid level sensor reaches 80cm, close valve B.
Based on processMode:
If processMode=false, start the mixing motor; after receiving the mixing complete signal, stop the mixing motor, open valve D, and the automatic process ends.
If processMode=true, open valve C; when the liquid level sensor reaches 100cm, close valve C, start the mixing motor; after receiving the mixing complete signal, stop the mixing motor, open valve D, and the automatic process ends.
变量类型方向说明
estopBOOLinputEmergency Stop Signal<br>FALSE: Emergency stop triggered<br>TRUE: No emergency stop triggered
opeModeBOOLinputOperation Mode<br>FALSE: Manual Operation Mode<br>TRUE: Automatic Operation Mode
valveAStartBOOLinputValve A Start Button
valveBStartBOOLinputValve B Start Button
valveCStartBOOLinputValve C Start Button
valveDStartBOOLinputValve D Start Button
mixMotorStartBOOLinputMixing Motor Start Button
autoStartBOOLinputAutomatic Start Button
processModeBOOLinputProcess Mode
levelSensorINTinputLiquid Level Sensor (unit cm)
mixMotorCompleteBOOLinputMixing Complete Signal
valveARunBOOLoutputValve A Run Command
valveBRunBOOLoutputValve B Run Command
valveCRunBOOLoutputValve C Run Command
valveDRunBOOLoutputValve D Run Command
mixMotorRunBOOLoutputMixing Motor Run Command

性质

  • S1 当急停触发（estop=FALSE）时，阀 A/B/C/D 与混合电机必须全部停止
    G(estop = FALSE -> (valveARun = FALSE AND valveBRun = FALSE AND valveCRun = FALSE AND valveDRun = FALSE AND mixMotorRun = FALSE))
  • S2 自动模式下阀 A、B、C 两两不得同时运行（进料同一时刻只有一个）
    G(opeMode = TRUE -> (NOT (valveARun = TRUE AND valveBRun = TRUE) AND NOT (valveARun = TRUE AND valveCRun = TRUE) AND NOT (valveBRun = TRUE AND valveCRun = TRUE)))
  • S3 自动模式下阀 D 运行时，阀 A/B/C 与混合电机必须停止（出料与前序设备互斥）
    G(opeMode = TRUE AND valveDRun = TRUE -> (valveARun = FALSE AND valveBRun = FALSE AND valveCRun = FALSE AND mixMotorRun = FALSE))
  • S4 自动模式下混合电机运行时，阀 A/B/C 必须关闭
    G(opeMode = TRUE AND mixMotorRun = TRUE -> (valveARun = FALSE AND valveBRun = FALSE AND valveCRun = FALSE))
  • S5 自动模式下混合完成信号的上升沿必须使混合电机停止并打开阀 D
    G(rising(mixMotorComplete) AND opeMode = TRUE AND mixMotorRun = TRUE -> (mixMotorRun = FALSE AND valveDRun = TRUE))
  • S6 手动按钮的边沿翻转记忆、换模式与急停时的复位、自动步序的推进顺序：由用例验证的时序/数据性质
    仿真性质
  • N10-S5 11.10补充：上一拍处于自动搅拌，本拍仍为自动且无急停时，完成信号有效则当拍停电机并开 D 阀。
    G(prev(opeMode) AND prev(mixMotorRun) AND opeMode AND estop AND mixMotorComplete -> (NOT mixMotorRun AND valveDRun))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：自动模式空闲时，启动沿首先开启 A 阀。
    G(((opeMode AND estop AND rising(autoStart) AND (NOT prev(opeMode) OR prev((NOT valveARun AND NOT valveBRun AND NOT valveCRun AND NOT valveDRun AND NOT mixMotorRun)))) -> valveARun))
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：自动A 转 B：上拍原设备已开，液位小于 50 时保持，达到阈值时切换。
    G((((estop AND opeMode AND prev(opeMode)) AND prev(valveARun)) -> ((levelSensor < 50 -> valveARun) AND (levelSensor >= 50 -> (NOT valveARun AND valveBRun)))))
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：自动B 转 C 或混合：上拍原设备已开，液位小于 80 时保持，达到阈值时切换。
    G((((estop AND opeMode AND prev(opeMode)) AND prev(valveBRun)) -> ((levelSensor < 80 -> valveBRun) AND (levelSensor >= 80 -> (NOT valveBRun AND ((valveCRun <-> processMode) AND (mixMotorRun <-> NOT processMode)))))))
  • N10-S11 11.10补充：自动C 转混合：上拍原设备已开，液位小于 100 时保持，达到阈值时切换。
    G((((estop AND opeMode AND prev(opeMode)) AND prev(valveCRun)) -> ((levelSensor < 100 -> valveCRun) AND (levelSensor >= 100 -> (NOT valveCRun AND mixMotorRun)))))
  • N10-S12 11.10补充：自动混合中，完成信号到来以前保持混合。
    G((((estop AND opeMode AND prev(opeMode)) AND prev(mixMotorRun) AND NOT mixMotorComplete) -> mixMotorRun))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-hard-02 · Flexible Processing Control · 64条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：9cf15fa70332aa15e84ab41eab519788248c1767d17679bcf946885341bee974

An automated production line consists of 5 workstations: numbered 1# to 5# from left to right. The 1# station is used for raw material loading, stations 2# to 4# are used for product processing, and the 5# station is used for finished product output and storage.
There is a motor in the production line, responsible for moving the product from one workstation to the next. Each workstation has a sensor (used to detect if the product has arrived).
Each product has a different sequence and number of processing steps, specified by the process sequence 'processSequence'. The processSequence is an array type, storing the sequence numbers of the workstations, and the system automatically sends the raw materials to the corresponding workstation for processing based on the station number.
Control requirements:
At the start of production, when the sensor at the 1# station (sensor1) detects the raw material, the start button at the 1# station (station1Start) is pressed. The system queries the first element of the process sequence 'processSequence', starts the conveyor to move the raw material to the corresponding workstation, and stops after delivery. Upon receiving the processing completion signal from the corresponding workstation (station2Complete to station4Complete), it continues to query the next element of the process sequence 'processSequence', determines the direction of the conveyor, and starts the conveyor (conveyorLeft, conveyorRight). This continues until the product is delivered to the 5# workstation.

processLength specifies the active sequence length, from 1 through 8, starting at processSequence[1]. The final active entry is output station 5; other active entries are processing stations 2 through 4. The caller supplies a sequence satisfying this contract.
变量类型方向说明
processLengthUSINTinputActive process-sequence length, 1..8; the final active entry must be station 5.
sensor1BOOLinputSensor at 1# station
sensor2BOOLinputSensor at 2# station
sensor3BOOLinputSensor at 3# station
sensor4BOOLinputSensor at 4# station
sensor5BOOLinputSensor at 5# station
station1StartBOOLinputStart button at 1# station
station2CompleteBOOLinputProcessing completion signal at 2# station
station3CompleteBOOLinputProcessing completion signal at 3# station
station4CompleteBOOLinputProcessing completion signal at 4# station
processSequenceARRAY[1..8] OF USINTinputProcessing sequence
conveyorLeftBOOLoutputConveyor turns left
conveyorRightBOOLoutputConveyor turns right

性质

  • S1 两条输送带任何时候都不得同时输出（方向互斥）。
    G(!(conveyorLeft AND conveyorRight))
  • S2 输入契约内，观察器判定空闲时输送带静止。
    G(__spec_contract AND __spec_idle -> NOT conveyorLeft AND NOT conveyorRight)
  • S3 输入契约内，等待加工完成时输送带静止。
    G(__spec_contract AND __spec_wait -> NOT conveyorLeft AND NOT conveyorRight)
  • S4 输入契约内，下一目标在右侧且未到位时正转。
    G(__spec_contract AND __spec_right -> conveyorRight AND NOT conveyorLeft)
  • S5 输入契约内，下一目标在左侧且未到位时反转。
    G(__spec_contract AND __spec_left -> conveyorLeft AND NOT conveyorRight)
  • S6 当前工位完成信号上升沿推进一工序后，输送输出须符合下一目标。只检验可观察效果，不断言私有 idx。
    G(__spec_contract AND __spec_complete -> conveyorLeft = __spec_left AND conveyorRight = __spec_right)
  • S7 输入契约内，有未到达的不同目标时必须输出一个输送方向。
    G(__spec_contract AND (__spec_left OR __spec_right) -> conveyorLeft OR conveyorRight)
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：合法工序中输送方向与本站、目标站的相对位置一致；目标仍为本站时两个方向都关闭。
    G((__spec_contract -> ((conveyorLeft <-> __spec_left) AND (conveyorRight <-> __spec_right))))
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：上电首拍的路线长度不在 1..8 时，拒绝启动，传送带两向都停。
    G((NOT prev(TRUE) AND (processLength < 1 OR processLength > 8)) -> (NOT conveyorLeft AND NOT conveyorRight))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-hard-03 · Recipe Management System · 19条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：55588de5f0731c5504251fd8601ebcf36ec6217739949c3b3b634e05bbd856bf

A factory's production line needs to produce a variety of products based on different recipes. The control center of the production line requires a recipe management system that allows operators to add, delete, modify, and query recipe data.
Control requirements:
Recipe data should include recipe number, raw material type, raw material ratio, and production temperature.
The system should allow input of new recipe data and store it in the recipe database.
The system should allow deletion or modification of existing recipe data by recipe number.
The system should allow querying recipe details by recipe number and output the recipe.
Notes:
1. Recipes are constructed using the ST_Recipe data type, which includes recipe number (recipeID), raw material type (ingredientType), raw material ratio (ingredientRatio), and production temperature (productionTemperature). The recipe database can store up to 10 recipes, constructed with ARRAY[1..10] OF ST_Recipe, where recipe numbers must not be duplicated, and a recipe number of 0 indicates an empty slot.
2. When a query recipe request signal (queryRecipe) is triggered, the system first compares the recipe number (recipeID) from the input recipe (recipeIn) with all recipes in the recipe database recipe. If the number does not exist, an error status (error=true) and error code (status=16#8001) are output. If the number exists, the recipe is output to the recipe query result (recipeQueryResult).
3. When an add recipe request signal (addRecipe) is triggered, the system first compares the recipe number (recipeID) from the input recipe (recipeIn) with all recipes in the recipe database (recipe). If a duplicate number exists, an error status (error=true) and error code (status=16#8002) are output. If there is no empty slot in the recipe database (recipe), an error status (error=true) and error code (status=16#8003) are output. If there are no errors, the input recipe (recipeIn) is stored in the recipe database (recipe), prioritizing the empty slot with the smallest array index.
4. When a delete recipe request signal (deleteRecipe) is triggered, the system first compares the recipe number (recipeID) from the input recipe (recipeIn) with all recipes in the recipe database (recipe). If the number does not exist, an error status (error=true) and error code (status=16#8004) are output. If the number exists, the recipe is deleted, i.e., the recipe number (recipeID) in the recipe database (recipe) is set to 0.
5. When a modify recipe request signal (modifyRecipe) is triggered, the system first compares the recipe number (recipeID) from the input recipe (recipeIn) with all recipes in the recipe database (recipe). If the number does not exist, an error status (error=true) and error code (status=16#8005) are output. If the number exists, the recipe is replaced with the input recipe (recipeIn).
变量类型方向说明
addRecipeBOOLinputAdd Recipe Request Signal
deleteRecipeBOOLinputDelete Recipe Request Signal
modifyRecipeBOOLinputModify Recipe Request Signal
queryRecipeBOOLinputQuery Recipe Request Signal
recipeInST_RecipeinputInput Recipe
recipeAddedBOOLoutputRecipe Added Success Indicator
recipeDeletedBOOLoutputRecipe Deleted Success Indicator
recipeModifiedBOOLoutputRecipe Modified Success Indicator
recipeQueryResultST_RecipeoutputRecipe Query Result Output
errorBOOLoutputError Status Indicator<br>FALSE: No Error Occurred<br>TRUE: Execution Error
statusWORDoutputStatus Code
recipeARRAY[1..10] OF ST_RecipeinoutRecipe Database

性质

  • S1 状态码非 0 的那一拍必须同时置起 error（非零状态码只用于报错）。
    G(status <> 0 -> error)
  • S2 error 为真时状态码只能是需求规定的五个错误码之一。
    G(error -> status = 32769 OR status = 32770 OR status = 32771 OR status = 32772 OR status = 32773)
  • S3 新增请求的上升沿必须落地：当拍要么 recipeAdded，要么报错，不会被静默丢掉。
    G(rising(addRecipe) -> recipeAdded OR error)
  • S4 删除请求的上升沿必须落地：当拍要么 recipeDeleted，要么报错。
    G(rising(deleteRecipe) -> recipeDeleted OR error)
  • S5 修改请求的上升沿必须落地：当拍要么 recipeModified，要么报错。
    G(rising(modifyRecipe) -> recipeModified OR error)
  • S6 查询成功的当拍 recipeQueryResult 必须是一条真实配方（配方号非 0），不会把空槽当结果输出。
    G(rising(queryRecipe) -> error OR recipeQueryResult.recipeID <> 0)

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

h03-16sequence1{"queryRecipe": true, "recipeIn": {"recipeID": 99, "ingredientType": 99, "ingredientRatio": 990.0, "productionTemperature": 595.0}}{}{"error": true, "status": 32769}部分查询库中不存在的 99 号 → error=TRUE、status=16#8001（负例）
h03-17sequence1{"deleteRecipe": true, "recipeIn": {"recipeID": 99, "ingredientType": 99, "ingredientRatio": 990.0, "productionTemperature": 595.0}}{}{"error": true, "status": 32772}部分删除库中不存在的 99 号 → error=TRUE、status=16#8004（负例）
h03-18sequence1{"modifyRecipe": true, "recipeIn": {"recipeID": 99, "ingredientType": 99, "ingredientRatio": 990.0, "productionTemperature": 595.0}}{}{"error": true, "status": 32773}部分修改库中不存在的 99 号 → error=TRUE、status=16#8005（负例）
h03-19sequence1{}{}{"recipeAdded": false, "recipeDeleted": false, "recipeModified": false, "error": false, "status": 0}部分所有请求信号都撤掉 → 成功指示与状态码回到 0、库不再变化（负例）
public-hard-04 · FIFO First-In-First-Out Queue · 56条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：48c310264e4ce28f3b89c8db9a57258f5816e3dacb5de600b151cbf58b79dfb6

Write a function block FB implementing a First-In-First-Out (FIFO) circular queue. Derive the queue capacity and element type from the caller-supplied buffer.

1. Each rising edge of enqueue requests one enqueue operation. If the queue is not full, append the current item value to its tail.
2. Each rising edge of dequeue requests one dequeue operation. If the queue is not empty, remove its front element and write that value back to item. The caller must not request enqueue and dequeue in the same invocation.
3. When reset is TRUE, empty the queue and set elementCount to zero. Reset takes priority over enqueue and dequeue; stored buffer values need not be overwritten.
4. Output the current number of valid elements through elementCount. Zero means the queue is empty; a count equal to the actual buffer array length means it is full.
5. Reject a dequeue request on an empty queue or an enqueue request on a full queue. Leave the queue and item unchanged and report the reason through status.
6. The queue is empty on its first invocation. Define head and tail positions and other internal state within the implementation; do not expose them as interface parameters.

Status codes:

- 16#0000: No error in this invocation, including idle, reset and successful operations.
- 16#8001: The dequeue request was rejected because the queue is empty.
- 16#8002: The enqueue request was rejected because the queue is full.
平台适配：item 固定为 DINT，buffer 是固定容量 DINT 数组；capacity 输入给定本次绑定的有效容量。算法仍须支持两种容量，复位/上下沿和状态码规则保持。 每次绑定后 capacity 在本次运行中保持不变；分别支持不同容量的运行，不要求运行中缩容或切换缓冲区。
变量类型方向说明
enqueueBOOLinputEnqueue request; one operation per rising edge.
dequeueBOOLinputDequeue request; one operation per rising edge.
resetBOOLinputTRUE resets the queue to empty; takes priority over enqueue and dequeue.
elementCountDINToutputCurrent number of valid elements, from zero through the queue capacity.
statusWORDoutputInvocation status: 16#0000 no error, 16#8001 dequeue from an empty queue, 16#8002 enqueue into a full queue.
itemDINTinoutMutable scalar reference: read its value when enqueuing; write the removed front value on successful dequeue. Its concrete type must match the array element type.
bufferARRAY[1..4] OF DINTinoutReference to a nonempty one-dimensional array with a single concrete element type, used as queue storage. Capacity is the actual array length.
capacityDINTinputLogical storage capacity; 1..4

性质

  • S1 有效元素个数永远落在 0..容量 之间，不会越界。
    G(elementCount >= 0 AND elementCount <= capacity)
  • S2 reset=TRUE 的那一拍队列必须为空（复位优先于入队/出队）。
    G(reset -> elementCount = 0)
  • S3 报 16#8001（空队出队被拒）的那一拍队列必须真的是空的。
    G(status = 32769 -> elementCount = 0)
  • S4 报 16#8002（满队入队被拒）的那一拍队列必须真的是满的。
    G(status = 32770 -> elementCount = capacity)
  • S5 FIFO 顺序、环形回绕、出队写回 item、容量随调用方数组长度变化这几条由用例逐拍验证（buffer/item 是 inout 的 VARIANT，模型检查器无法为它们取初值，可达状态里容量恒为 0，因而这些性质只能靠仿真覆盖）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S1 11.10补充：有效元素个数介于 0 和调用方缓冲区的实际元素个数之间。
    G(elementCount >= 0 AND elementCount <= (capacity))
  • N10-S4 11.10补充：返回满队错误时，计数必须等于调用方缓冲区的实际元素个数。
    G(status = 32770 -> elementCount = (capacity))
  • N10-S6 11.10补充：合法入队上升沿检查成功/拒绝时的计数与精确状态码。
    G(((NOT reset AND rising(enqueue) AND NOT dequeue) -> ((capacity > prev(elementCount) -> (status = 0 AND elementCount = prev(elementCount) + 1)) AND (NOT (capacity > prev(elementCount)) -> (status = 32770 AND elementCount = prev(elementCount))))))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-hard-05 · Mechanical Arm Control · 88条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：cb5e006f22f402a59a34103b2ecf0d34f0aabbeb3f2728ef65766e039982f647

Design a PLC program to control an automated mechanical arm that performs handling tasks on the production line. The mechanical arm has six basic actions: down, up, left, right, grip, and release. The operating modes of the mechanical arm include four types: manual, single step, single cycle, and continuous.
Control requirements:
1. Manual mode: Allows the operator to control each action of the mechanical arm individually through the control panel, provided it is not yet in position. For example, pressing the down button (DownButton=1), if not at the down position (DownLimitSwitch=0), then move down (MoveDown=1); if at the down position (DownLimitSwitch=1), stop moving down. Releasing the down button also stops the movement down.
2. Single step mode: The mechanical arm automatically executes one action according to the preset program, and the operator needs to confirm after each action is completed to execute the next action.
The initial position of the mechanical arm is at the top left.
When the start button is pressed once, the mechanical arm executes the down command MoveDown, and stops after reaching the down position.
When the start button is pressed again, the mechanical arm executes the grip command ActivateGrip, and stops after gripping.
When the start button is pressed again, the mechanical arm executes the up command MoveUp, and stops after reaching the up position.
When the start button is pressed, the mechanical arm executes the right command MoveRight, and stops after reaching the right position.
When the start button is pressed, the mechanical arm executes the down command MoveDown, and stops after reaching the down position.
When the start button is pressed, the mechanical arm executes the release command ActivateRelease, and stops after releasing.
When the start button is pressed again, the mechanical arm executes the up command MoveUp, and stops after reaching the up position.
When the start button is pressed again, the mechanical arm executes the left command MoveLeft, and stops after reaching the left position.
Then starts the cycle again from the beginning
3. Single cycle mode: The mechanical arm automatically completes a full handling cycle, including all preset actions, and stops at the end of the cycle waiting for the operator's instruction.
The initial position of the mechanical arm is at the top left, when the start button is pressed:
The mechanical arm first moves down, then grips after reaching the down position, moves up after gripping, moves right after reaching the up position, moves down after reaching the right position, releases after reaching the down position, moves up after releasing, moves left after reaching the up position, and stops after reaching the left position.
4. Continuous mode: The initial position of the mechanical arm is at the top left, when the start button is pressed, the mechanical arm continuously executes the handling cycle of the single cycle mode.
5. The emergency stop function can stop all actions of the mechanical arm at any time.

Use GripLimitSwitch and ReleaseLimitSwitch as the completion feedback for gripping and releasing respectively. The caller keeps the four mode-selection inputs mutually exclusive.
变量类型方向说明
ManualModeBOOLinputManual mode
SingleStepModeBOOLinputSingle step mode
SingleCycleModeBOOLinputSingle cycle mode
ContinuousModeBOOLinputContinuous mode
EmergencyStopBOOLinputEmergency stop signal<br>FALSE: Emergency stop triggered<br>TRUE: No emergency stop triggered
StartBOOLinputStart operation
DownButtonBOOLinputDown button
UpButtonBOOLinputUp button
LeftButtonBOOLinputLeft button
RightButtonBOOLinputRight button
GripButtonBOOLinputGrip button
ReleaseButtonBOOLinputRelease button
DownLimitSwitchBOOLinputDown position limit switch
UpLimitSwitchBOOLinputUp position limit switch
LeftLimitSwitchBOOLinputLeft position limit switch
RightLimitSwitchBOOLinputRight position limit switch
GripLimitSwitchBOOLinputGrip position limit switch
ReleaseLimitSwitchBOOLinputRelease position limit switch
MoveDownBOOLoutputDown command
MoveUpBOOLoutputUp command
MoveLeftBOOLoutputLeft command
MoveRightBOOLoutputRight command
ActivateGripBOOLoutputGrip command
ActivateReleaseBOOLoutputRelease command

性质

  • S1 同一条轴上的两个相反动作永远不会同时输出。
    G(NOT (MoveDown AND MoveUp))
  • S2 左右两个相反动作永远不会同时输出。
    G(NOT (MoveLeft AND MoveRight))
  • S3 抓取与释放永远不会同时输出。
    G(NOT (ActivateGrip AND ActivateRelease))
  • S4 急停（EmergencyStop=FALSE）时六个动作输出全部为 0，压过任何模式与按钮。
    G(NOT EmergencyStop -> (NOT MoveDown AND NOT MoveUp AND NOT MoveLeft AND NOT MoveRight AND NOT ActivateGrip AND NOT ActivateRelease))
  • S5 手动模式下动作只能由对应按钮驱动（下行动作必须 DownButton 有效）。
    G(ManualMode AND MoveDown -> DownButton)
  • S6 手动模式下已到位的方向不能再输出动作（到位即停）。
    G(ManualMode AND DownLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveDown)
  • S7 三种自动模式的预置程序顺序（下→抓→上→右→下→放→上→左，单步逐次确认、单循环跑完一轮停下、连续到第 8 步后环回第 1 步）、模式切换即停、急停冻结后从同一步恢复，均由 32 条用例逐拍验证（这些性质涉及程序步计数，用规约表达会退化成对内部实现细节的约束，故用仿真覆盖）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S6 11.10补充：手动模式下，每个方向或抓放动作的到位开关必须当拍禁止对应输出。
    G(ManualMode -> ((DownLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveDown) AND (UpLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveUp) AND (LeftLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveLeft) AND (RightLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveRight) AND (GripLimitSwitch -> NOT ActivateGrip) AND (ReleaseLimitSwitch -> NOT ActivateRelease)))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：手动模式无急停，按钮有效、反向按钮未按、相应限位未到时，对应动作须在当拍输出。
    G(ManualMode AND EmergencyStop -> ((DownButton AND NOT UpButton AND NOT DownLimitSwitch -> MoveDown) AND (UpButton AND NOT DownButton AND NOT UpLimitSwitch -> MoveUp) AND (LeftButton AND NOT RightButton AND NOT LeftLimitSwitch -> MoveLeft) AND (RightButton AND NOT LeftButton AND NOT RightLimitSwitch -> MoveRight) AND (GripButton AND NOT ReleaseButton AND NOT GripLimitSwitch -> ActivateGrip) AND (ReleaseButton AND NOT GripButton AND NOT ReleaseLimitSwitch -> ActivateRelease)))
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：手动模式中，每个动作都必须有对应按钮按下。
    G((ManualMode -> ((MoveDown -> DownButton) AND (MoveUp -> UpButton) AND (MoveRight -> RightButton) AND (MoveLeft -> LeftButton) AND (ActivateGrip -> GripButton) AND (ActivateRelease -> ReleaseButton))))
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：自动动作在自己的限位有效时禁止开启。
    G(((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) -> ((DownLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveDown) AND (UpLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveUp) AND (RightLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveRight) AND (LeftLimitSwitch -> NOT MoveLeft) AND (GripLimitSwitch -> NOT ActivateGrip) AND (ReleaseLimitSwitch -> NOT ActivateRelease))))
  • N10-S11 11.10补充：从上电或手动状态进入自动模式，起始启动沿先执行向下动作。
    G(((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND (NOT prev(TRUE) OR prev(ManualMode)) AND rising(Start) AND NOT DownLimitSwitch) -> MoveDown))
  • N10-S12 11.10补充：单步模式下，看不到当前动作的到位反馈就应继续原动作。
    G((((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND SingleStepMode) AND prev((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND SingleStepMode))) -> (((prev(MoveDown) AND NOT DownLimitSwitch) -> MoveDown) AND ((prev(MoveUp) AND NOT UpLimitSwitch) -> MoveUp) AND ((prev(MoveRight) AND NOT RightLimitSwitch) -> MoveRight) AND ((prev(MoveLeft) AND NOT LeftLimitSwitch) -> MoveLeft) AND ((prev(ActivateGrip) AND NOT GripLimitSwitch) -> ActivateGrip) AND ((prev(ActivateRelease) AND NOT ReleaseLimitSwitch) -> ActivateRelease))))
  • N10-S13 11.10补充：单步模式完成本动作当拍停止，等待下次确认。
    G(((((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND SingleStepMode) AND prev((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND SingleStepMode))) AND ((prev(MoveDown) AND DownLimitSwitch) OR (prev(MoveUp) AND UpLimitSwitch) OR (prev(MoveRight) AND RightLimitSwitch) OR (prev(MoveLeft) AND LeftLimitSwitch) OR (prev(ActivateGrip) AND GripLimitSwitch) OR (prev(ActivateRelease) AND ReleaseLimitSwitch))) -> (NOT MoveDown AND NOT MoveUp AND NOT MoveRight AND NOT MoveLeft AND NOT ActivateGrip AND NOT ActivateRelease)))
  • N10-S14 11.10补充：循环模式下，看不到当前动作的到位反馈就应继续原动作。
    G((((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND NOT SingleStepMode AND (SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND prev((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND NOT SingleStepMode AND (SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode))) AND (SingleCycleMode <-> prev(SingleCycleMode))) -> (((prev(MoveDown) AND NOT DownLimitSwitch) -> MoveDown) AND ((prev(MoveUp) AND NOT UpLimitSwitch) -> MoveUp) AND ((prev(MoveRight) AND NOT RightLimitSwitch) -> MoveRight) AND ((prev(MoveLeft) AND NOT LeftLimitSwitch) -> MoveLeft) AND ((prev(ActivateGrip) AND NOT GripLimitSwitch) -> ActivateGrip) AND ((prev(ActivateRelease) AND NOT ReleaseLimitSwitch) -> ActivateRelease))))
  • N10-S15 11.10补充：循环中抓取、向右或释放到位后，下一动作尚未到位时分别执行上、下、上。
    G((((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND NOT SingleStepMode AND (SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND prev((((NOT ManualMode AND (SingleStepMode OR SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode)) AND EmergencyStop) AND NOT SingleStepMode AND (SingleCycleMode OR ContinuousMode))) AND (SingleCycleMode <-> prev(SingleCycleMode))) -> (((prev(ActivateGrip) AND GripLimitSwitch AND NOT UpLimitSwitch) -> MoveUp) AND ((prev(MoveRight) AND RightLimitSwitch AND NOT DownLimitSwitch) -> MoveDown) AND ((prev(ActivateRelease) AND ReleaseLimitSwitch AND NOT UpLimitSwitch) -> MoveUp))))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

编号组拍数有效输入初态预期断言范围说明

public-hard-06 · Production Control · 49条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：c3d4f66e05857c72a5af1f65e7823460ba8bccadd528be426e44dc37c8b04692

An automated production line consists of 5 workstations: material loading station, cleaning station, weighing station, firing station, and finished product station. The production line has a motor responsible for moving products from one workstation to the next, the sequence of the line is from the material loading station, cleaning station, weighing station, firing station, to the finished product station. Each workstation has a sensor (used to detect if the product has arrived).
Control requirements:
1. Mode Switching: The mode switch (Mode) is used to switch between manual and automatic modes of the production line. 0 = manual mode, 1 = automatic mode.
2. Manual Mode: In manual mode, the forward rotation button (ForwardButton) controls the forward rotation of the motor, and the reverse rotation button (ReverseButton) controls the reverse rotation of the motor. Forward and reverse rotation operations need to be interlocked and cannot be performed simultaneously.
3. Automatic Mode:
   - When the material arrives at the material loading station, the SensorFeed detects the material, the motor starts forward rotation, and moves the material to the cleaning station.
   - When the material arrives at the cleaning station, the SensorWash detects the material, the motor stops, and upon receiving the signal washComplete after cleaning is finished, the motor starts forward rotation, moving the material to the weighing station.
   - When the material arrives at the weighing station, the SensorWeight detects the material, the motor stops, and upon receiving weightComplete after weighing is complete, the material is judged to be qualified based on the weight value. If the weight value is greater than or equal to 100 and less than or equal to 200, it is qualified, otherwise not.
     If the material is unqualified, the rejection command kick is initiated.
     If the material is qualified, the firing temperature is calculated based on the weight value. The calculation rule is that the weight value from 100 to 200 linearly corresponds to the temperature value from 500 to 550. The firing temperature firingTemp is output. At the same time, the motor starts forward rotation, moving the material to the firing station.
   - When the product arrives at the firing station, the SensorFiring detects the material, the motor stops, and upon receiving the signal firingComplete after firing is finished, the motor starts forward rotation, moving the product to the finished product station.
   - When the product arrives at the finished product station, the SensorOutput detects the product, the motor stops, and the completion light (CompletionLight) turns on, indicating that the product processing is complete. When the product is taken away, i.e., the SensorOutput does not detect the product, the completion light (CompletionLight) turns off.
变量类型方向说明
ModeBOOLinputUsed to switch the production line between manual and automatic modes, 0 = manual mode, 1 = automatic mode
ForwardButtonBOOLinputForward rotation button
ReverseButtonBOOLinputReverse rotation button
SensorFeedBOOLinputSensor at the material loading station detecting the arrival of materials
SensorWashBOOLinputSensor at the cleaning station detecting the arrival of materials
SensorWeightBOOLinputSensor at the weighing station detecting the arrival of materials
SensorFiringBOOLinputSensor at the firing station detecting the arrival of materials
SensorOutputBOOLinputSensor at the finished product station detecting the arrival of products
washCompleteBOOLinputSignal indicating completion of cleaning
weightCompleteBOOLinputSignal indicating completion of weighing
weightREALinputWeight value
firingCompleteBOOLinputSignal indicating completion of firing
MotorForwardBOOLoutputOutput for motor forward rotation
MotorReverseBOOLoutputOutput for motor reverse rotation
kickBOOLoutputRejection output
CompletionLightBOOLoutputIndicator light for product processing completion
firingTempREALoutputFiring temperature

性质

  • S1 电机正转与反转永远互斥，不会同时输出。
    G(NOT (MotorForward AND MotorReverse))
  • S2 自动模式下电机只正转、不反转。
    G(Mode -> NOT MotorReverse)
  • S3 剔除指令只在不合格判定的那一拍给出，且给出时电机不转（不在传送中剔除）。
    G(kick -> NOT MotorForward AND NOT MotorReverse)
  • S4 烧结温度只可能是 0.0（尚未产生合格品）或落在 500.0~550.0 区间内。
    G(firingTemp = 0.0 OR (firingTemp >= 500.0 AND firingTemp <= 550.0))
  • S5 手动模式下正转只能由 ForwardButton 驱动。
    G(NOT Mode AND MotorForward -> ForwardButton)
  • S6 手动模式下反转只能由 ReverseButton 驱动。
    G(NOT Mode AND MotorReverse -> ReverseButton)
  • S7 完成灯点亮期间成品传感器必须仍检测到产品（产品被取走灯就灭）。
    G(CompletionLight -> SensorOutput)
  • S8 五工位流水顺序（上料→清洗→称重→烧结→成品）、合格判定端点 100/200、不合格只剔除不放行、烧结温度线性映射 500~550 由用例逐拍验证（数值映射属于算术性质，用规约表达不划算，故以仿真覆盖）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：手动模式单独按正/反转按钮时驱动对应方向。
    G((NOT Mode -> (((ForwardButton AND NOT ReverseButton) -> MotorForward) AND ((ReverseButton AND NOT ForwardButton) -> MotorReverse))))
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：自动入口发现进料立即正转；紧接到达清洗、清洗完成、到达称重时依次停车、启动、停车。
    G(((((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)) -> MotorForward) AND ((prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)) AND (Mode AND SensorWash)) -> NOT MotorForward) AND ((prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed))) AND prev((Mode AND SensorWash)) AND (Mode AND washComplete)) -> MotorForward) AND ((prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)))) AND prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash))) AND prev((Mode AND washComplete)) AND (Mode AND SensorWeight)) -> NOT MotorForward)))
  • N10-S11 11.10补充：首件无等待到达称重并完成时，按闭区间判合格，合格时线性设温并送烧结，不合格时立即剔除。
    G(((prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed))))) AND prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash)))) AND prev(prev((Mode AND washComplete))) AND prev((Mode AND SensorWeight)) AND (Mode AND weightComplete)) -> (((weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0) -> (NOT kick AND MotorForward AND firingTemp = 500.0 + (weight - 100.0) * 0.5)) AND (NOT (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0) -> (kick AND NOT MotorForward)))))
  • N10-S12 11.10补充：任意扫描拍出现剔除或新的烧结温度时，必须有对应的称重结果，设温符合线性换算。
    G(((kick -> (Mode AND weightComplete AND NOT (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0))) AND (firingTemp <> prev(firingTemp) -> (Mode AND weightComplete AND (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0) AND firingTemp = 500.0 + (weight - 100.0) * 0.5))))
  • N10-S13 11.10补充：首件无等待由称重进入烧结，在到位、烧结完成、成品到位时依次停、走、停并亮灯。
    G((((prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash))))) AND prev(prev(prev((Mode AND washComplete)))) AND prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWeight))) AND prev(((Mode AND weightComplete) AND (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0))) AND (Mode AND SensorFiring)) -> NOT MotorForward) AND ((prev(prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed))))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash)))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND washComplete))))) AND prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWeight)))) AND prev(prev(((Mode AND weightComplete) AND (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0)))) AND prev((Mode AND SensorFiring)) AND (Mode AND firingComplete)) -> MotorForward) AND ((prev(prev(prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)))))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash))))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND washComplete)))))) AND prev(prev(prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWeight))))) AND prev(prev(prev(((Mode AND weightComplete) AND (weight >= 100.0 AND weight <= 200.0))))) AND prev(prev((Mode AND SensorFiring))) AND prev((Mode AND firingComplete)) AND (Mode AND SensorOutput)) -> (NOT MotorForward AND CompletionLight))))
  • N10-S14 11.10补充：首件从上料正常进入清洗，清洗完成后的下一拍尚未到达称重站时，应继续正向输送。
    G((prev(prev(prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorFeed)))) AND prev(prev((Mode AND SensorWash))) AND prev((Mode AND washComplete)) AND Mode AND NOT SensorWeight) -> MotorForward)

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-medium-01 · Multi-Pump Switching Control System · 12条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：1a70bee1e2f2b075eae45ad0fa0006bca28e3a4e46f7465defcfd8b214984006

In an industrial water supply system, there are 5 pumps, each of which can be controlled independently. The system needs to support both manual and automatic operating modes.
Based on the value of the mode variable, switch to the corresponding operating mode.
In manual mode, control the start and stop of the selected pump based on the value of pumpGroup[1..5].selection.
In automatic mode, compare the priority of each pump, and automatically select the 3 pumps with higher priority for water supply according to the method of three in use and two on standby.
变量类型方向说明
modeBOOLinputFALSE: Manual mode<br>TRUE: Automatic mode
startBOOLinputStart button, used to start the manually selected or automatically selected pump
stopBOOLinputStop button, used to stop all pumps
pumpGroupARRAY[1..5] OF ST_PumpinoutFive pump records passed by reference. Read priority and selection; write only RunComd.

性质

  • S1 当停止按钮为 TRUE 时，五台泵的运行命令必须全部为 FALSE（停止优先于手动/自动方式）。
    G(stop -> (pumpGroup[1].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd = FALSE))
  • S2 当启动按钮为 FALSE 时，任何一台泵都不得收到运行命令。
    G(NOT start -> (pumpGroup[1].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd = FALSE AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd = FALSE))
  • S3 手动方式下，未被选中的泵必须收到停机命令（selection=FALSE → RunComd=FALSE）。
    G((NOT mode AND NOT pumpGroup[1].selection) -> pumpGroup[1].RunComd = FALSE)
  • S4 自动方式按 priority 选前三台运行、其余两台备用停机；优先级相等时下标小者优先（三用两备规则，由 m01-2/6/8/10 用例验证的数据性质）。
    仿真性质
  • S5 手动方式下 RunComd = start AND selection（逐台启停，由 m01-1/5/9 用例验证的数据性质）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S6 11.10补充：手动启动且未停机时，每台泵的命令分别跟随自身 selection。
    G((start AND NOT stop AND NOT mode) -> ((pumpGroup[1].RunComd <-> pumpGroup[1].selection) AND (pumpGroup[2].RunComd <-> pumpGroup[2].selection) AND (pumpGroup[3].RunComd <-> pumpGroup[3].selection) AND (pumpGroup[4].RunComd <-> pumpGroup[4].selection) AND (pumpGroup[5].RunComd <-> pumpGroup[5].selection)))
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：自动运行时恰有三台泵运行。
    G((start AND NOT stop AND mode) -> ((pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd) OR (NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND pumpGroup[5].RunComd)))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：自动运行时任一在役泵的优先级均不低于任一备用泵。
    G((start AND NOT stop AND mode) -> (((pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[1].priority >= pumpGroup[2].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[1].priority >= pumpGroup[3].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[1].priority >= pumpGroup[4].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[1].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[1].priority >= pumpGroup[5].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[2].priority >= pumpGroup[1].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[2].priority >= pumpGroup[3].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[2].priority >= pumpGroup[4].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[2].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[2].priority >= pumpGroup[5].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[3].priority >= pumpGroup[1].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[3].priority >= pumpGroup[2].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[3].priority >= pumpGroup[4].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[3].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[3].priority >= pumpGroup[5].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[4].priority >= pumpGroup[1].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[4].priority >= pumpGroup[2].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[4].priority >= pumpGroup[3].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[4].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[5].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[4].priority >= pumpGroup[5].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[5].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[1].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[5].priority >= pumpGroup[1].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[5].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[2].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[5].priority >= pumpGroup[2].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[5].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[3].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[5].priority >= pumpGroup[3].priority) AND ((pumpGroup[5].RunComd AND NOT pumpGroup[4].RunComd) -> pumpGroup[5].priority >= pumpGroup[4].priority)))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-medium-02 · Analog Batch Processing · 13条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：b2c9948a66c6e46900d9addbd996d1370510d546b7404078c7998997211de88a

Write a PLC function block that processes analog inputs in batches and converts them into engineering values.

1. analogValues supplies the raw-value array. channels supplies per-channel settings and receives conversion results. count specifies how many channels to process from the beginning of the arrays. The caller must provide count from zero through the smaller of the two array lengths. Match channels by ordinal position starting at each array's own lower bound. Read analogValues without modifying it.
2. Each channel is enabled by its enable field and converted according to measuringMode. Read configuration fields without modifying them; write only retVal and outputValue.
3. For measuringMode 0, perform non-temperature linear scaling: when bipolar is FALSE, map raw values 0..27648 to loLIM..hiLIM; when TRUE, map -27648..27648 to loLIM..hiLIM.
4. For measuringMode 1, perform standard-temperature conversion: outputValue is one tenth of the raw analog value.
5. For measuringMode 2, perform climatic-temperature conversion: outputValue is one hundredth of the raw analog value.
6. If measuringMode is not 0, 1 or 2, set that channel's retVal to 16#8001 and skip its conversion.
7. If hiLIM is less than or equal to loLIM, set that channel's retVal to 16#8002 and skip its conversion.
变量类型方向说明
countDINTinputNumber of channels to be converted
analogValuesARRAY[1..8] OF INTinoutVariable-length analog array passed by reference to preserve its bounds. Read-only to the function block; no element may be modified. 容量上限 8（平台不解析 ARRAY[*] 的运行时长度）；实际有效通道数由 count 给出，按 1..count 取值。
channelsARRAY[1..8] OF ST_AnalogChannelinoutChannel records passed by reference. Configuration fields are read-only; write only retVal and outputValue. 容量上限 8（平台不解析 ARRAY[*] 的运行时长度）；实际有效通道数由 count 给出，按 1..count 取值。

性质

  • S5 count 小于 1 时不得处理任何通道，channels 的 retVal/outputValue 必须保持上一拍的值。
    G(count < 1 -> (channels[1].retVal = prev(channels[1].retVal) & channels[1].outputValue = prev(channels[1].outputValue) & channels[8].retVal = prev(channels[8].retVal)))
  • S6 未使能通道的 retVal 不得被改写（enable=FALSE 的通道整条跳过）。
    G(!channels[1].enable -> channels[1].retVal = prev(channels[1].retVal))
  • S1 analogValues 是只读输入量，任何周期都不得被改写；未使能通道的 retVal/outputValue 必须保持原值。
    仿真性质
  • S2 measuringMode 不在 0/1/2 时该通道 retVal=16#8001 且跳过转换；hiLIM<=loLIM 时 retVal=16#8002 且跳过转换。
    仿真性质
  • S3 mode0 线性标定：bipolar=FALSE 时 0..27648 线性映射到 loLIM..hiLIM，bipolar=TRUE 时 -27648..27648 映射到 loLIM..hiLIM。
    仿真性质
  • S4 mode1 标准温度 outputValue=raw/10，mode2 气候温度 outputValue=raw/100；count 指定从各数组下界起处理的通道数并按序号对应（容量上限 8）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：对实际选中并启用的全部八个通道依次核对模式、量程错误及正常状态。

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-medium-03 · Calculate Moving Average · 17条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：211c924eb5517ade7439c4e877285acbdf38662d6e6763c1037b2d5d8a81b537

Write a function block FB that calculates and updates a moving arithmetic mean. The moving arithmetic mean is a method of averaging over consecutive data points, where each new data point replaces the oldest data point, and then the average is recalculated.

1. When cyclicExecution is TRUE, a new data value is automatically read and the moving average average is updated every scan cycle.
2. Provide an external trigger signal trigger, which reads a new data value and updates the moving average upon each rising edge.
3. Provide a reset function reset, which resets the moving average calculation when the reset signal is TRUE. If the window size is less than 1 or greater than 100, output an error status and status code.

The status parameter indicates the execution status of the program:
- 16#0000: Execution successful
- 16#8200: Window length setting error, please set a value between 1 and 100.
变量类型方向说明
cyclicExecutionBOOLinputWhen TRUE, periodic reading is performed, and trigger has no effect
triggerBOOLinputExternal trigger signal, reads value upon each rising edge
valueLREALinputNewly read data value
windowSizeINTinputThe window size for the moving average, which must be between 1 and 100
resetBOOLinputReset signal, resets the moving average calculation when TRUE
averageLREALoutputThe moving average
windowSizeReachedBOOLoutputFALSE: Maximum window width not yet reached TRUE: Maximum window width has been reached
errorBOOLoutputFALSE: No error occurred TRUE: An error occurred during the execution of FB
statusWORDoutputStatus code

性质

  • S1 窗口尺寸小于 1 或大于 100 时必须置 error=TRUE。
    G((windowSize < 1 | windowSize > 100) -> error)
  • S2 错误状态置位时状态码必须为 16#8200（33280）。
    G(error -> status = 33280)
  • S3 无错误时状态码必须为 16#0000。
    G(!error -> status = 0)
  • S4 复位信号为 TRUE 时，移动平均必须清零。
    G(reset -> average = 0.0)
  • S5 复位信号为 TRUE 时，窗口未满标志必须清除。
    G(reset -> !windowSizeReached)
  • S6 average 等于最近 min(累计样本数, windowSize) 个样本的算术平均；累计样本数小于 windowSize 时 windowSizeReached=FALSE，达到时 TRUE；重复值不合并，窗口变化后只保留最近 windowSize 个样本。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：窗口在 1..100 范围时必须正常。
    G((windowSize >= 1 AND windowSize <= 100) -> NOT error)
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：尺寸一且此拍有有效采样时必须宣告满窗。
    G((NOT reset AND windowSize = 1 AND (cyclicExecution OR rising(trigger))) -> windowSizeReached)

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

编号组拍数有效输入初态预期断言范围说明
s1sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 10.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 10.0, "windowSizeReached": false, "error": false, "status": 0}部分窗口=2、周期模式首个样本：窗口未满，平均=10、reached=FALSE、无错误
s2sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 20.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 15.0, "windowSizeReached": true}部分第二个样本 20：窗口填满，平均=(10+20)/2=15、reached 置 TRUE
s3sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 30.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 25.0}部分第三个样本 30：FIFO 淘汰最旧 10，平均=(20+30)/2=25
s4sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 30.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 30.0}部分重复值 30：连续两次相同样本，平均=(30+30)/2=30（重复值不合并）
s5sequence1{"cyclicExecution": false, "trigger": true, "value": 40.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 35.0}部分周期模式关、trigger 上升沿：本拍采一个样本，平均=(30+40)/2=35
s6sequence1{"cyclicExecution": false, "trigger": true, "value": 50.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 35.0}部分trigger 保持 TRUE 无新上升沿：不采样，平均保持 35（只认边沿）
s7sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": true, "value": 60.0, "windowSize": 2, "reset": false}{}{"average": 50.0}部分cyclicExecution=TRUE 且 trigger 同时为 TRUE：周期模式优先且只采一次，平均=(40+60)/2=50
s8sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 100.0, "windowSize": 4, "reset": false}{}{"average": 57.5, "windowSizeReached": true}部分窗口由 2 改为 4：取最近 4 个样本 30,40,60,100，平均=57.5；累计样本 7>=4 故 reached=TRUE
s9sequence1{"cyclicExecution": true, "trigger": false, "value": 5.0, "windowSize": 101, "reset": false}{}{"average": 57.5, "windowSizeReached": true, "error": true, "status": 33280}完整窗口=101 越上界：error=TRUE、status=16#8200(33280)，不采样、统计值保持上一次 57.5

public-medium-04 · Temperature Logic Control · 74条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：024d1c0319d70fcb5b829cd6d8b637ac05c7d64bb818d5c6832a4ce049f23de8

Write a function block FB to implement a temperature control logic. The program should include the following functions:
1. Read the value of the temperature sensor through an analog input module.
2. Compare the read temperature value TemperatureSensorInput with the set temperature value SetTemperature.
3. If the actual temperature is lower than the set temperature, start the heater HeaterOutput.
4. If the actual temperature is higher than the set temperature, turn off the heater HeaterOutput.
5. Each scan represents 1 second. The fifth consecutive heating-demand scan triggers protection and already turns the heater off. Cooling starts on the next scan: two scans remain off; the third clears protection and re-evaluates the current input. Cooling advances independently of sensor readings and faults.
6. Provide status indication, displaying the current temperature, set temperature, heater status, and whether overheating protection is triggered.
7. Provide temperature sensor fault judgment, if the read temperature value TemperatureSensorInput is less than 0 or greater than 100, it is considered a sensor fault, and the heater should be turned off.
本修订明确逐拍验收边界：每拍1秒。读数合法（0..100，含边界）且严格低于设定才有加热需求；等于设定或读数故障均关断并清连续加热计数。
连续需求第5拍触发过热保护，当拍已关断；从下一拍计冷却，前两拍保持关断，第3拍解除保护并按当拍读数重新决策。冷却计数独立于温度和故障；若解除当拍重新加热，该拍为下一轮第1拍。
连续低温时第1..4拍开，第5..7拍关，第8拍重新开。显示直通当前输入，HeaterStatus跟随HeaterOutput。
变量类型方向说明
TemperatureSensorInputREALinputThe input value of the temperature sensor, i.e., the actual temperature
SetTemperatureREALinputThe set temperature value
CurrentTemperatureREALoutputDisplay of the current temperature
SetTempDisplayREALoutputDisplay of the set temperature
HeaterStatusBOOLoutputDisplay of the heater status
OverheatProtectionBOOLoutputDisplay of the overheating protection status
HeaterOutputBOOLoutputThe control output of the heater

性质

  • S1 传感器读数低于 0 或高于 100 时，加热输出必须为 FALSE。
    G((TemperatureSensorInput < 0.0 | TemperatureSensorInput > 100.0) -> !HeaterOutput)
  • S2 过热保护有效期间，加热输出必须被强制关断。
    G(OverheatProtection -> !HeaterOutput)
  • S3 读数等于或高于设定温度时关断加热。
    G((TemperatureSensorInput >= SetTemperature & TemperatureSensorInput <= 100.0) -> !HeaterOutput)
  • S4 无故障、无过热保护且读数低于设定温度时，加热输出必须为 TRUE（过载触发保护的当拍除外）。
    G((TemperatureSensorInput < SetTemperature & TemperatureSensorInput >= 0.0 & TemperatureSensorInput <= 100.0 & !OverheatProtection) -> HeaterOutput)
  • S5 过热保护连续 3 个周期后必须解除。
    G((OverheatProtection & X(OverheatProtection) & X(X(OverheatProtection))) -> X(X(X(!OverheatProtection))))
  • S6 CurrentTemperature 等于传感器读数、SetTempDisplay 等于设定温度、HeaterStatus 跟随 HeaterOutput；连续加热 5 个扫描周期触发过热保护（1 个扫描周期代表 1s，平台无 TIME 类型）。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S3 11.10补充：实际读数达到或超过设定值时，加热输出为 FALSE，包括解除保护当拍。
    G(TemperatureSensorInput >= SetTemperature -> !HeaterOutput)
  • N10-S6 11.10补充：显示值跟随当拍温度和设定值，状态指示跟随实际加热输出。
    G((CurrentTemperature = TemperatureSensorInput) AND (SetTempDisplay = SetTemperature) AND (HeaterStatus <-> HeaterOutput))

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。

public-medium-05 · Automated Production Line Control · 26条

人工结论：待刘晏铭填写；参考源码SHA-256：7e32a8fedbcd66e36c5e5b9be1f18ebd9a9bcb179a93139a7bebc34f77603947

A simple automated production line consists of three workstations: Workstation A, Workstation B, and Workstation C. The product needs to go through these three stations in sequence to be considered fully completed. There is a motor in the production line that is responsible for moving the product from one workstation to the next. The sequence of the line is from Workstation A to Workstation B, and then to Workstation C. Each workstation has a sensor (used to detect if the product has arrived) and a button (used to trigger the signal that the station's processing is complete).
Control requirements:
1. Mode Switching: The mode switch (Mode) is used to switch between manual and automatic modes of the production line.
2. Manual Mode: In manual mode, the forward rotation button (ForwardButton) controls the forward rotation of the motor, and the reverse rotation button (ReverseButton) controls the reverse rotation of the motor. Forward and reverse rotation operations need to be interlocked and cannot be performed simultaneously.
3. Automatic Mode:
   - When the product arrives at Workstation A, SensorA detects the product, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonA, the motor starts forward rotation to move the product to Workstation B.
   - When the product arrives at Workstation B, SensorB detects the product, the motor stops, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonB, the motor starts forward rotation to move the product to Workstation C.
   - When the product arrives at Workstation C, SensorC detects the product, the motor stops, and after the operator triggers the processing complete signal via ButtonC, the completion indicator light (CompletionLight) turns on, indicating that the product processing is complete. When the product is taken away, i.e., SensorC no longer detects the product, the completion indicator light (CompletionLight) turns off.
变量类型方向说明
ModeBOOLinputUsed to switch the production line between manual and automatic modes, 0=manual mode, 1=automatic mode
ForwardButtonBOOLinputForward rotation button
ReverseButtonBOOLinputReverse rotation button
SensorABOOLinputSensor A
SensorBBOOLinputSensor B
SensorCBOOLinputSensor C
ButtonABOOLinputButton A
ButtonBBOOLinputButton B
ButtonCBOOLinputButton C
MotorForwardBOOLoutputMotor forward rotation output
MotorReverseBOOLoutputMotor reverse rotation output
CompletionLightBOOLoutputIndicator light for product processing completion

性质

  • S1 正转与反转输出互锁，任何周期都不得同时为 TRUE。
    G(!(MotorForward & MotorReverse))
  • S2 正转只可能出现在自动模式或手动按下正转按钮时。
    G(MotorForward -> (Mode | ForwardButton))
  • S3 反转只在手动模式且按下反转按钮时输出。
    G(MotorReverse -> (!Mode & ReverseButton))
  • S4 完成灯亮的前提是自动模式且 C 工位传感器检测到产品。
    G(CompletionLight -> (SensorC & Mode))
  • S5 产品被取走后（下一周期 SensorC 不再检测），完成灯必须在下一周期熄灭。
    G(CompletionLight & X(!SensorC) -> X(!CompletionLight))
  • S6 自动模式按 A->B->C 顺序推进：A 工位检测到产品且 ButtonA 后正转，B/C 传感器检测到即停，ButtonB 后继续正转，C 工位 ButtonC 后完成灯亮、产品取走即灭；手动模式下正转/反转按钮互锁，两者同时按下都不动作，切到手动会清空自动流程。
    仿真性质
  • N10-S7 11.10补充：手动模式恰有一个方向按钮按下时，电机必须按该方向运行。
    G(NOT Mode -> (((ForwardButton AND NOT ReverseButton) -> MotorForward) AND ((ReverseButton AND NOT ForwardButton) -> MotorReverse)))
  • N10-S8 11.10补充：上电或从手动进入自动，A 工位有料且确认后开始向 B 输送。
    G((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorA AND ButtonA) -> MotorForward)
  • N10-S9 11.10补充：从自动开始经 A 确认、B 到位/确认、C 到位/确认的连续五拍应点亮完成灯。
    G(((Mode AND SensorC AND ButtonC) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorC) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorB AND ButtonB) AND prev(((Mode AND SensorB) AND prev((Mode AND NOT prev(Mode) AND SensorA AND ButtonA))))))))) -> CompletionLight)
  • N10-S10 11.10补充：C 工位仍有已完成产品且保持自动时，灯持续点亮。
    G((Mode AND SensorC AND prev(CompletionLight)) -> CompletionLight)
  • N10-S11 11.10补充：自动方式发出新的正转命令时，必须有 A 到位并确认，或 B 到位并确认。
    G((Mode AND rising(MotorForward)) -> ((SensorA AND ButtonA) OR (SensorB AND ButtonB)))
  • N10-S12 11.10补充：仍在自动输送且下游 B、C 都未到位时，已运行的正转电机必须保持，不得只在启动拍发一个脉冲。
    G((Mode AND prev(Mode AND MotorForward) AND NOT SensorB AND NOT SensorC) -> MotorForward)

用例

相同sequence组连续；不同组新实例；single每条独立。输入包含本行常量，未写BOOL按FALSE处理；初态在本行扫描前设置。cycles为本行实际扫描次数。断言范围列注明是否强制完整输出。
